# Loot

Includes each built-in loot table, condition, function, entry type, context parameter set, integer and float context provider type and named provider. Generic integer and float provider registries are in foundations.md.

Environment checks defer five shared value families to the environment-attributes model owned by worldgen or level settings: particles, ambient particle lists, background music, ambient sounds and mob-spawn settings. Their decoders return `Unsupported(environment_attribute.value_codec.<attribute>)`; no built-in loot table uses these value families.

| ID | Feature | Vanilla source | Test | Vanilla | Ferraloom |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| loot.context.parameters | Validate required and optional context parameters and reject unavailable context values | `LootParams; LootContext; LootContextParamSets` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | - | todo |
| loot.context.random-sequence | Use persistent named random sequences and seed overrides for loot generation | `LootTable#getRandomItems; RandomSequences` | `loot::tests::random_sequence_seeds_and_saved_words_match_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.context.random-sequence-dirty | Mark named sequence saved data dirty only on random source operations, preserving clean get/reset/clear/default changes | `RandomSequences.DirtyMarkingRandomSource` | `loot::tests::named_sequence_dirty_marking_matches_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.pool.rolls-bonus | Compute pool rolls and bonus rolls using provider values and luck | `LootPool#addRandomItems` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.pool.weight-quality | Choose weighted entries with luck-adjusted quality and expand alternatives and sequences | `LootPool; LootPoolEntryContainer` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.pool.conditions-functions | Evaluate entry and pool conditions and apply composed functions in their defined order | `LootPool; SingleEntryContainerBase` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.table.references | Resolve nested tables and prevent reference recursion | `LootTable; LootContext#pushVisitedElement` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | - | todo |
| loot.table.stack-splitting | Split oversized generated item stacks to stack limits before insertion | `LootTable#createStackSplitter` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.container.fill | Shuffle generated items and available slots and split stacks to fill containers | `LootTable#fill` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | pass | partial |
| loot.container.delayed-generation | Generate container loot once with the saved table id, seed and player luck | `RandomizableContainerBlockEntity#unpackLootTable` |  | - | todo |
| loot.entity.player-credit | Expose last damage source, killer, attacking player and looting-sensitive context | `LivingEntity#dropFromLootTable` |  | - | todo |
| loot.block.tool-explosion | Expose block state, tool, block entity and explosion radius for block loot | `Block#getDrops` |  | - | todo |
| loot.fishing.context | Expose fishing rod, hook position, open-water and player luck context | `FishingHook#retrieve` |  | - | todo |
| loot.providers.references | Evaluate context value providers and named providers with context-key validation | `ContextIntProvider; ContextFloatProvider` | `loot::tests::synthetic_tables_and_providers_match_real_java` | pass | partial |
